Title: Letters on Spiritual Subjects, and Divers Occasions; sent to relations and friends; by one who has tasted that the Lord is gracious
Creator: Anne Dutton (1692-1765, per the host); the title page names the writer only as 'one who has tasted that the Lord is gracious'
Publisher: London: printed, and sold by John Oswald, at the Rose and Crown in the Poultry, near the Mansion-house; and Ebenezer Gardner, at Milton's Head in Gracechurch Street
Publication Date: 1740
Rights: Public Domain (published 1740, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Source: Internet Archive item lettersonspiritu01dutt (https://archive.org/details/lettersonspiritu01dutt), the item's own lettersonspiritu01dutt_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1740. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e0d1ea9468f5b3ae580b8b11dd98c3a598c6d311b4e29f0cf0ba2085118b6aa3.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary scan; readable with the long s as f; the library's stamps precede the title page. The host records it as volume 1.
Content note: Spiritual letters by a Baptist woman writer, addressed to relations and friends in their troubles; one of the few women's theological voices of eighteenth-century English Dissent in her own words.
